Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Versioned Stanza Manager for Shell Config Files

## Context

Shell completion setup in `setup.go` currently blindly appends text to rc files (`~/.bashrc`, `~/.zshrc`, `~/.config/fish/config.fish`). There's no way to surgically update or remove just the lines we added. This makes upgrades messy and uninstall incomplete (uninstall doesn't currently touch rc files at all).

The goal: wrap our managed text in versioned, demarcated blocks ("stanzas") so we can find, update, or remove them cleanly.

## Stanza Format

```bash
# BEGIN entire-cli (v1)
source <(entire completion bash)
# END entire-cli
```

- `# BEGIN <name> (v<version>)` opens the block
- `# END <name>` closes the block
- Version is an integer, bumped when stanza content changes
- Name identifies the stanza (allows multiple independent stanzas in the same file in the future)

## Implementation

### 1. New file: `cmd/entire/cli/stanza.go`

Three pure functions operating on string content (no file I/O):

```go
// FindStanza locates a named stanza in content.
// Returns version, body, found. If not found, version=-1, body="", found=false.
func FindStanza(content, name string) (version int, body string, found bool)

// UpsertStanza inserts or replaces a named stanza. Returns new file content.
func UpsertStanza(content string, name string, version int, body string) string

// RemoveStanza removes a named stanza. Returns content unchanged if not found.
func RemoveStanza(content, name string) string
```

Implementation uses line-by-line scanning (no regex):
- Scan for line matching `# BEGIN <name> (v<digit+>)`
- Scan forward for `# END <name>`
- Extract/replace/remove everything between (inclusive of markers)

### 2. New file: `cmd/entire/cli/stanza_test.go`

Test cases:
- Find stanza (present, absent, different version)
- Upsert into empty content (append)
- Upsert when stanza already exists (replace in-place, preserving surrounding content)
- Upsert when no stanza exists yet (append)
- Remove stanza (present, absent)
- Preserve surrounding content on upsert/remove
- Edge cases: no trailing newline, stanza at beginning/end of file, multiple stanzas

### 3. Update `setup.go`

**Constants:**
```go
const stanzaName = "entire-cli"
const stanzaVersion = 1
```

**Replace `isCompletionConfigured()` → `completionStatus()`:**

Returns a status indicating what's in the rc file:
- `statusCurrent` — stanza exists at current version → nothing to do
- `statusStale` — stanza exists at older version → prompt to update
- `statusLegacy` — old non-stanza format detected (`# Entire CLI shell completion` + completion line) → prompt to migrate
- `statusMissing` — nothing found → prompt to install

**Replace `appendShellCompletion()` → `installShellCompletion()`:**
1. Read file content (or empty string if doesn't exist)
2. If legacy format present, remove old comment + completion line first
3. Call `UpsertStanza(content, stanzaName, stanzaVersion, completionLine)`
4. Write full file back (same permissions: dir 0o700, file 0o644)

**New `removeShellCompletion(rcFile)` function:**
1. Read file content
2. Try `RemoveStanza(content, stanzaName)` for new format
3. Also detect and remove legacy format if present
4. Write file back if content changed

**Update `promptShellCompletion()`** (called from `curl-bash-post-install`):
1. Detect shell → get rcFile + completionLine
2. Check `completionStatus(rcFile)`:
   - `statusCurrent` → print "already configured", return
   - `statusStale` / `statusLegacy` / `statusMissing` → prompt user (as currently done)
3. If user says yes → call `installShellCompletion()`

**Update `runUninstall()`:**
- After existing cleanup steps, detect shell and call `removeShellCompletion()` to remove the stanza from the user's rc file
- Show in the confirmation list: `"- Shell completion (<rcFile>)"`

### 4. Update tests

**`cmd/entire/cli/stanza_test.go`** (new) — pure function tests for Find/Upsert/Remove

**`cmd/entire/cli/setup_test.go`** (modify):
- Update/add tests for `completionStatus()` — all four statuses
- Add tests for `installShellCompletion()` — writes stanza, migrates legacy
- Add tests for `removeShellCompletion()` — removes stanza, removes legacy
- Verify uninstall calls removeShellCompletion

## Files

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/stanza.go` | **New** — `FindStanza`, `UpsertStanza`, `RemoveStanza` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/stanza_test.go` | **New** — unit tests for stanza functions |
| `cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` | **Modify** — use stanza functions, add remove on uninstall |
| `cmd/entire/cli/setup_test.go` | **Modify** — update/add completion tests |

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint` — passes
2. `mise run test` — all unit tests pass
3. `mise run test:ci` — all tests including integration pass

   139→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   140→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   141→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   142→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, selectedStrategy string, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   143→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   144→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   145→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   146→		internalStrategy = mapped
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Validate the strategy exists
   150→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   156→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   157→	if err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   161→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   162→	} else {
   163→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Setup .entire directory
   167→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→	if dirCreated {
   172→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   176→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   179→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   180→	}
   181→	// Update the specific fields
   182→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   183→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   184→	settings.Enabled = true
   185→
   186→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   187→	if skipPushSessions {
   188→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   189→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   190→		}
   191→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   195→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   196→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   197→		f := false
   198→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   202→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   203→	if err != nil {
   204→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   205→	}
   206→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   207→
   208→	if showNotification {
   209→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   210→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	if shouldUseLocal {
   214→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   215→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   216→		}
   217→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   218→	} else {
   219→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   220→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   221→		}
   222→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   226→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   227→	if err != nil {
   228→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   229→	}
   230→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   231→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   232→	} else {
   233→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   237→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   238→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Show success message with display name
   242→	displayName := selectedStrategy
   243→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy]; ok {
   244→		displayName = dn
   245→	}
   246→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", displayName)
   247→
   248→	return nil
   249→}

   251→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   252→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   253→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   254→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   255→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Using %s strategy (use --strategy to change)\n\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   256→
   257→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   258→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   263→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   264→	} else {
   265→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Setup .entire directory
   269→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   272→	}
   273→	if dirCreated {
   274→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   278→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   279→	if err != nil {
   280→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   281→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   282→	}
   283→	// Update the specific fields
   284→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   285→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   286→	settings.Enabled = true
   287→
   288→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   289→	if skipPushSessions {
   290→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   291→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   292→		}
   293→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   297→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   298→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Determine which settings file to write to (interactive prompt if settings.json exists)
   302→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   305→	}
   306→	shouldUseLocal, err := promptSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return err
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	if shouldUseLocal {
   312→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   313→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   314→		}
   315→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   316→	} else {
   317→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   318→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   319→		}
   320→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   324→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   327→	}
   328→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   329→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   330→	} else {
   331→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   335→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   336→	if err != nil {
   337→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   338→	}
   339→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   340→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Show success message with display name
   344→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   345→
   346→	return nil
   347→}

   349→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   350→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   351→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	settings.Enabled = true
   357→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   358→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   362→	return nil
   363→}
   364→
   365→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   366→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   367→	if err != nil {
   368→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	settings.Enabled = false
   372→
   373→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   374→	if useProjectSettings {
   375→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   376→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   377→		}
   378→	} else {
   379→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   380→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   381→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   382→		}
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   386→	return nil
   387→}
   388→
   389→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   390→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   391→
   392→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   393→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   394→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   395→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   396→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		// Default to enabled on error
   399→		return false
   400→	}
   401→	if !enabled {
   402→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   403→		return true
   404→	}
   405→	return false
   406→}
   407→
   408→// setupClaudeCodeHook sets up Claude Code hooks.
   409→// This is a convenience wrapper that uses the agent package.
   410→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   411→func setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) {
   412→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get claude-code agent: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   418→	if !ok {
   419→		return 0, errors.New("claude-code agent does not support hooks")
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   423→	if err != nil {
   424→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install claude-code hooks: %w", err)
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	return count, nil
   428→}
   429→
   430→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   431→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   432→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(agentName agent.AgentName, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   433→	ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
   434→	if err != nil {
   435→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", agentName)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   439→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   440→	if !ok {
   441→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Install hooks
   445→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	if count == 0 {
   451→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   452→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   453→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   454→		}
   455→		fmt.Println(msg)
   456→	} else {
   457→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", count, ag.Description())
   458→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   459→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   460→		}
   461→		fmt.Println(msg)
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	// Update settings to store the strategy
   465→	settings, _ := LoadEntireSettings() //nolint:errcheck // settings defaults are fine
   466→	settings.Enabled = true
   467→	if localDev {
   468→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   472→	if skipPushSessions {
   473→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   474→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   475→		}
   476→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Set strategy if provided
   480→	if strategyName != "" {
   481→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   482→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   483→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   484→			internalStrategy = mapped
   485→		}
   486→		// Validate the strategy exists
   487→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   488→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   489→		}
   490→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   494→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   495→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   496→		f := false
   497→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   501→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   505→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   506→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/sessions branch, etc.)
   510→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   511→	if err != nil {
   512→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   513→	}
   514→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   515→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	return nil
   519→}
   520→
   521→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   522→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   523→	if useLocal && useProject {
   524→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   525→	}
   526→	return nil
   527→}
   528→
   529→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   530→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   531→// - The --local and --project flags
   532→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   533→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   534→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   535→	if useLocal {
   536→		return true, false
   537→	}
   538→
   539→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   540→	if useProject {
   541→		return false, false
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   545→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   546→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   547→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   548→		return true, true
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   552→	return false, false
   553→}
   554→
   555→// Settings target options for interactive prompt
   556→const (
   557→	settingsTargetProject = "project"
   558→	settingsTargetLocal   = "local"
   559→)
   560→
   561→// promptSettingsTarget interactively asks the user where to save settings
   562→// when settings.json already exists and no flags were provided.
   563→// Returns (useLocal, error).
   564→func promptSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, error) {
   565→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   566→	if useLocal {
   567→		return true, nil
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   571→	if useProject {
   572→		return false, nil
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// Check if settings file exists
   576→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   577→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   578→		// Settings file doesn't exist - create it (no prompt needed)
   579→		return false, nil
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Settings file exists - prompt user
   583→	var selected string
   584→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   585→		huh.NewGroup(
   586→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   587→				Title("Project settings already exist. Where should settings be saved?").
   588→				Options(
   589→					huh.NewOption("Update project settings (settings.json)", settingsTargetProject),
   590→					huh.NewOption("Use local settings (settings.local.json, gitignored)", settingsTargetLocal),
   591→				).
   592→				Value(&selected),
   593→		),
   594→	)
   595→
   596→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   597→		return false, fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   598→	}
   599→
   600→	return selected == settingsTargetLocal, nil
   601→}
   602→
   603→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   604→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   605→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) {
   606→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   607→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   608→	if err != nil {
   609→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   610→	}
   611→
   612→	// Check if directory already exists
   613→	created := false
   614→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   615→		created = true
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Create .entire directory
   619→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   620→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   621→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   625→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   626→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	return created, nil
   630→}

   632→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   633→func setupGitHook() error {
   634→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   635→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   636→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   637→	if err != nil {
   638→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   639→	}
   640→	return nil
   641→}

   644→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {

   656→
   657→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   658→	return &cobra.Command{
   659→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   660→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   661→		Hidden: true,
   662→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   663→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   664→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   665→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   666→			}
   667→			return nil
   668→		},

   671→
   672→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   673→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   674→
   675→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   676→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   677→
   678→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   679→// user's current shell.
   680→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   681→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   682→	if err != nil {
   683→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   684→	}
   685→
   686→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   687→	switch {
   688→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   689→		return "Zsh",
   690→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   691→			"source <(entire completion zsh)",
   692→			nil
   693→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   694→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   695→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   696→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   697→		}
   698→		return "Bash",
   699→			bashRC,
   700→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   701→			nil
   702→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   703→		return "Fish",
   704→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   705→			"entire completion fish | source",
   706→			nil
   707→	default:
   708→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   709→	}
   710→}
   711→
   712→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   713→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   714→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   715→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   716→	if err != nil {
   717→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   718→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   719→			return nil
   720→		}
   721→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   722→	}
   723→
   724→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   725→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   726→		return nil
   727→	}
   728→
   729→	var selected string
   730→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   731→		huh.NewGroup(
   732→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   733→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   734→				Options(
   735→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   736→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   737→				).
   738→				Value(&selected),
   739→		),
   740→	)
   741→
   742→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   743→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   744→		return nil
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	if selected != "yes" {
   748→		return nil
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   752→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   753→	}
   754→
   755→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   756→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   757→
   758→	return nil
   759→}
   760→
   761→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   762→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   763→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   764→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   765→	if err != nil {
   766→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   767→	}
   768→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   769→}
   770→
   771→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   772→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   773→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
   774→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
   775→	}
   776→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   777→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   778→	if err != nil {
   779→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
   780→	}
   781→	defer f.Close()
   782→
   783→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
   784→	if err != nil {
   785→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
   786→	}
   787→	return nil
   788→}
   789→
   790→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   791→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   792→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   793→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   794→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   795→	if !telemetryFlag {
   796→		f := false
   797→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   798→		return nil
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	// Skip if already asked
   802→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   803→		return nil
   804→	}
   805→
   806→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   807→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   808→		f := false
   809→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   810→		return nil
   811→	}
   812→
   813→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   814→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   815→		huh.NewGroup(
   816→			huh.NewConfirm().
   817→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   818→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   819→				Affirmative("Yes").
   820→				Negative("No").
   821→				Value(&consent),
   822→		),
   823→	)
   824→
   825→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   826→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
   830→	return nil
   831→}
   832→
   833→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   834→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   835→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   836→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   837→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   838→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   839→	}
   840→
   841→	// Gather counts for display
   842→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   843→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   844→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   845→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   846→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   847→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   848→
   849→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   850→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   851→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
   852→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   853→		return nil
   854→	}
   855→
   856→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
   857→	if !force {
   858→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
   859→		if entireDirExists {
   860→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
   861→		}
   862→		if gitHooksInstalled {
   863→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   864→		}
   865→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
   866→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
   867→		}
   868→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
   869→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
   870→		}
   871→		switch {
   872→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
   873→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
   874→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
   875→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
   876→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
   877→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
   878→		}
   879→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   880→
   881→		var confirmed bool
   882→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   883→			huh.NewGroup(
   884→				huh.NewConfirm().
   885→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
   886→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
   887→					Negative("Cancel").
   888→					Value(&confirmed),
   889→			),
   890→		)
   891→
   892→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   893→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   894→		}
   895→
   896→		if !confirmed {
   897→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
   898→			return nil
   899→		}
   900→	}
   901→
   902→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
   903→
   904→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
   905→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
   906→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
   907→	}
   908→
   909→	// 2. Remove git hooks
   910→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
   911→	if err != nil {
   912→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
   913→	} else if removed > 0 {
   914→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
   915→	}
   916→
   917→	// 3. Remove session state files
   918→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
   919→	if err != nil {
   920→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
   921→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
   922→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
   923→	}
   924→
   925→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
   926→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   927→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
   928→	} else if entireDirExists {
   929→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
   930→	}
   931→
   932→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
   933→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
   934→	if err != nil {
   935→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
   936→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
   937→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
   938→	}
   939→
   940→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
   941→	return nil
   942→}
   943→
   944→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
   945→func countSessionStates() int {
   946→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   947→	if err != nil {
   948→		return 0
   949→	}
   950→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   951→	if err != nil {
   952→		return 0
   953→	}
   954→	return len(states)
   955→}
   956→
   957→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
   958→func countShadowBranches() int {
   959→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
   960→	if err != nil {
   961→		return 0
   962→	}
   963→	return len(branches)
   964→}
   965→
   966→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
   967→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
   968→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   969→	if err != nil {
   970→		return false
   971→	}
   972→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   973→	if !ok {
   974→		return false
   975→	}
   976→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
   977→}
   978→
   979→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
   980→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
   981→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
   982→	if err != nil {
   983→		return false
   984→	}
   985→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   986→	if !ok {
   987→		return false
   988→	}
   989→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
   990→}
   991→
   992→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
   993→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
   994→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   995→	if err != nil {
   996→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
   997→	}
   998→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
   999→	return err == nil
  1000→}
  1001→
  1002→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1003→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1004→	var errs []error
  1005→
  1006→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1007→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1008→	if err == nil {
  1009→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1010→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1011→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1012→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1013→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1014→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1015→			}
  1016→		}
  1017→	}
  1018→
  1019→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1020→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1021→	if err == nil {
  1022→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1023→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1024→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1025→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1026→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1027→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1028→			}
  1029→		}
  1030→	}
  1031→
  1032→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1033→}
  1034→
  1035→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1036→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1037→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1038→	if err != nil {
  1039→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1040→	}
  1041→
  1042→	// Count states before removing
  1043→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1044→	if err != nil {
  1045→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1046→	}
  1047→	count := len(states)
  1048→
  1049→	// Remove the entire directory
  1050→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1051→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1052→	}
  1053→
  1054→	return count, nil
  1055→}
  1056→
  1057→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1058→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1059→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1060→	if err != nil {
  1061→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1062→	}
  1063→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1064→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1065→	}
  1066→	return nil
  1067→}
  1068→
  1069→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1070→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1071→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1072→	if err != nil {
  1073→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1074→	}
  1075→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1076→		return 0, nil
  1077→	}
  1078→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1079→	return len(deleted), err
  1080→}
  1081→

   342→func TestRunEnableWithStrategy_PreservesExistingSettings(t *testing.T) {
   343→	setupTestRepo(t)
   344→
   345→	// Create initial settings with strategy_options (like push enabled)
   346→	initialSettings := `{
   347→		"strategy": "manual-commit",
   348→		"enabled": true,
   349→		"strategy_options": {
   350→			"push": true,
   351→			"some_other_option": "value"
   352→		}
   353→	}`
   354→	writeSettings(t, initialSettings)
   355→
   356→	// Run enable with a different strategy

   358→	err := runEnableWithStrategy(&stdout, "auto-commit", false, false, false, true, false, false, false)
   359→	if err != nil {
   360→		t.Fatalf("runEnableWithStrategy() error = %v", err)
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Load the saved settings and verify strategy_options were preserved
   364→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		t.Fatalf("LoadEntireSettings() error = %v", err)
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Strategy should be updated
   370→	if settings.Strategy != "auto-commit" {
   371→		t.Errorf("Strategy should be 'auto-commit', got %q", settings.Strategy)
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// strategy_options should be preserved
   375→	if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   376→		t.Fatal("strategy_options should be preserved, but got nil")
   377→	}
   378→	if settings.StrategyOptions["push"] != true {
   379→		t.Errorf("strategy_options.push should be true, got %v", settings.StrategyOptions["push"])
   380→	}
   381→	if settings.StrategyOptions["some_other_option"] != "value" {
   382→		t.Errorf("strategy_options.some_other_option should be 'value', got %v", settings.StrategyOptions["some_other_option"])
   383→	}
   384→}

   474→	setupTestRepo(t)
   475→
   476→	// Create .entire directory (required for git hooks)
   477→	writeSettings(t, testSettingsEnabled)
   478→
   479→	// Install git hooks
   480→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   481→		t.Fatalf("InstallGitHook() error = %v", err)
   482→	}
   483→
   484→	// Verify hooks are installed
   485→	if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   486→		t.Fatal("git hooks should be installed before uninstall")
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   490→	err := runUninstall(&stdout, &stderr, true)
   491→	if err != nil {
   492→		t.Fatalf("runUninstall() error = %v", err)
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Verify hooks are removed
   496→	if strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   497→		t.Error("git hooks should be removed after uninstall")
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	output := stdout.String()
   501→	if !strings.Contains(output, "Removed git hooks") {
   502→		t.Errorf("Expected output to mention removed git hooks, got: %s", output)
   503→	}
   504→}
   505→
   506→func TestRunUninstall_NotAGitRepo(t *testing.T) {
   507→	// Create a temp directory without git init
   508→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   509→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
   510→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
   511→
   512→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   513→	err := runUninstall(&stdout, &stderr, true)
   514→
   515→	// Should return an error (silent error)
   516→	if err == nil {
   517→		t.Fatal("runUninstall() should return error for non-git directory")
   518→	}
   519→
   520→	// Should print message to stderr
   521→	errOutput := stderr.String()
   522→	if !strings.Contains(errOutput, "Not a git repository") {
   523→		t.Errorf("Expected error message about not being a git repo, got: %s", errOutput)
   524→	}
   525→}
   526→
   527→func TestCheckEntireDirExists(t *testing.T) {
   528→	setupTestDir(t)
   529→
   530→	// Should be false when directory doesn't exist
   531→	if checkEntireDirExists() {
   532→		t.Error("checkEntireDirExists() should return false when .entire doesn't exist")
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	// Create the directory
   536→	if err := os.MkdirAll(paths.EntireDir, 0o755); err != nil {

   539→
   540→	// Should be true now
   541→	if !checkEntireDirExists() {
   542→		t.Error("checkEntireDirExists() should return true when .entire exists")
   543→	}
   544→}

   566→func TestRemoveEntireDirectory(t *testing.T) {
   567→	setupTestDir(t)
   568→
   569→	// Create .entire directory with some files
   570→	entireDir := paths.EntireDir
   571→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(entireDir, "subdir"), 0o755); err != nil {
   572→		t.Fatalf("Failed to create .entire/subdir: %v", err)
   573→	}
   574→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, "test.txt"), []byte("test"), 0o644); err != nil {

   581→	}

   588→
   589→func TestShellCompletionTarget(t *testing.T) {
   590→	tests := []struct {
   591→		name             string
   592→		shell            string
   593→		createBashProf   bool
   594→		wantShell        string
   595→		wantRCBase       string // basename of rc file
   596→		wantCompletion   string
   597→		wantErrUnsupport bool
   598→	}{
   599→		{
   600→			name:           "zsh",
   601→			shell:          "/bin/zsh",
   602→			wantShell:      "Zsh",
   603→			wantRCBase:     ".zshrc",
   604→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion zsh)",
   605→		},
   606→		{
   607→			name:           "bash_no_profile",
   608→			shell:          "/bin/bash",
   609→			wantShell:      "Bash",
   610→			wantRCBase:     ".bashrc",
   611→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion bash)",
   612→		},
   613→		{
   614→			name:           "bash_with_profile",
   615→			shell:          "/bin/bash",
   616→			createBashProf: true,
   617→			wantShell:      "Bash",
   618→			wantRCBase:     ".bash_profile",
   619→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion bash)",
   620→		},
   621→		{
   622→			name:             "fish_unsupported",
   623→			shell:            "/usr/bin/fish",
   624→			wantErrUnsupport: true,
   625→		},
   626→		{
   627→			name:             "empty_shell",
   628→			shell:            "",
   629→			wantErrUnsupport: true,
   630→		},
   631→	}
   632→
   633→	for _, tt := range tests {
   634→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   635→			home := t.TempDir()
   636→			t.Setenv("HOME", home)
   637→			t.Setenv("SHELL", tt.shell)
   638→
   639→			if tt.createBashProf {
   640→				if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile"), []byte(""), 0o644); err != nil {
   641→					t.Fatal(err)
   642→				}
   643→			}
   644→
   645→			shellName, rcFile, completion, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   646→
   647→			if tt.wantErrUnsupport {
   648→				if err != errUnsupportedShell {
   649→					t.Fatalf("got err=%v, want errUnsupportedShell", err)
   650→				}
   651→				return
   652→			}
   653→			if err != nil {
   654→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   655→			}
   656→			if shellName != tt.wantShell {
   657→				t.Errorf("shellName = %q, want %q", shellName, tt.wantShell)
   658→			}
   659→			if filepath.Base(rcFile) != tt.wantRCBase {
   660→				t.Errorf("rcFile base = %q, want %q", filepath.Base(rcFile), tt.wantRCBase)
   661→			}
   662→			wantRC := filepath.Join(home, tt.wantRCBase)
   663→			if rcFile != wantRC {
   664→				t.Errorf("rcFile = %q, want %q", rcFile, wantRC)
   665→			}
   666→			if completion != tt.wantCompletion {
   667→				t.Errorf("completion = %q, want %q", completion, tt.wantCompletion)
   668→			}
   669→		})
   670→	}
   671→}
   672→

   677→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   678→		t.Fatalf("removeEntireDirectory() should not error when directory doesn't exist: %v", err)
   679→	}
   680→}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/paul/src/entireio/completion-stanza-manager/cmd/entire/cli/stanza.go",
  "content": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// FindStanza locates a named stanza in content.\n// Returns version, body, found. If not found, version=-1, body=\"\", found=false.\nfunc FindStanza(content, name string) (version int, body string, found bool) {\n\tbeginPrefix := \"# BEGIN \" + name + \" (v\"\n\tendLine := \"# END \" + name\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\tinStanza := false\n\tstanzaVersion := -1\n\tvar bodyLines []string\n\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif !inStanza {\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, beginPrefix) && strings.HasSuffix(trimmed, \")\") {\n\t\t\t\t// Extract version number\n\t\t\t\tvStr := trimmed[len(beginPrefix) : len(trimmed)-1]\n\t\t\t\tv, err := strconv.Atoi(vStr)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tinStanza = true\n\t\t\t\tstanzaVersion = v\n\t\t\t\tbodyLines = nil\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tif trimmed == endLine {\n\t\t\t\treturn stanzaVersion, strings.Join(bodyLines, \"\\n\"), true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tbodyLines = append(bodyLines, line)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn -1, \"\", false\n}\n\n// UpsertStanza inserts or replaces a named stanza. Returns new file content.\nfunc UpsertStanza(content string, name string, version int, body string) string {\n\tbeginPrefix := \"# BEGIN \" + name + \" (v\"\n\tendLine := \"# END \" + name\n\tbeginLine := fmt.Sprintf(\"# BEGIN %s (v%d)\", name, version)\n\n\tstanzaBlock := beginLine + \"\\n\" + body + \"\\n\" + endLine\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\tvar result []string\n\tinStanza := false\n\treplaced := false\n\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif !inStanza {\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, beginPrefix) && strings.HasSuffix(trimmed, \")\") {\n\t\t\t\t// Validate it has a version number\n\t\t\t\tvStr := trimmed[len(beginPrefix) : len(trimmed)-1]\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := strconv.Atoi(vStr); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, line)\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tinStanza = true\n\t\t\t\t// Add the replacement stanza\n\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, stanzaBlock)\n\t\t\t\treplaced = true\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, line)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tif trimmed == endLine {\n\t\t\t\tinStanza = false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Skip old stanza lines (already replaced)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !replaced {\n\t\t// Append to end, ensuring a blank line separator\n\t\ttrimmedContent := strings.TrimRight(content, \"\\n\")\n\t\tif trimmedContent == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn stanzaBlock + \"\\n\"\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn trimmedContent + \"\\n\\n\" + stanzaBlock + \"\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.Join(result, \"\\n\")\n}\n\n// RemoveStanza removes a named stanza. Returns content unchanged if not found.\nfunc RemoveStanza(content, name string) string {\n\tbeginPrefix := \"# BEGIN \" + name + \" (v\"\n\tendLine := \"# END \" + name\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\tvar result []string\n\tinStanza := false\n\tfound := false\n\t// Track index where stanza starts to remove preceding blank line\n\tstanzaStartIdx := -1\n\n\tfor _, line := range lines {\n\t\ttrimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)\n\t\tif !inStanza {\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, beginPrefix) && strings.HasSuffix(trimmed, \")\") {\n\t\t\t\t// Validate it has a version number\n\t\t\t\tvStr := trimmed[len(beginPrefix) : len(trimmed)-1]\n\t\t\t\tif _, err := strconv.Atoi(vStr); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, line)\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tinStanza = true\n\t\t\t\tfound = true\n\t\t\t\tstanzaStartIdx = len(result)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, line)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tif trimmed == endLine {\n\t\t\t\tinStanza = false\n\t\t\t\t// Remove trailing blank line after stanza if present\n\t\t\t\t// (will be handled below)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Skip stanza body lines\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif !found {\n\t\treturn content\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove blank line before the stanza if it exists\n\tif stanzaStartIdx > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(result[stanzaStartIdx-1]) == \"\" {\n\t\tresult = append(result[:stanzaStartIdx-1], result[stanzaStartIdx:]...)\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up trailing empty lines — keep at most one trailing newline\n\toutput := strings.Join(result, \"\\n\")\n\toutput = strings.TrimRight(output, \"\\n\")\n\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t}\n\n\treturn output\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestFindStanza_Present(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# some config\nexport FOO=bar\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v2)\nsource <(my-tool completion bash)\n# END my-tool\n\nexport BAZ=qux\n`\n\tversion, body, found := FindStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif !found {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected stanza to be found\")\n\t}\n\tif version != 2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"version = %d, want 2\", version)\n\t}\n\tif body != \"source <(my-tool completion bash)\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"body = %q, want %q\", body, \"source <(my-tool completion bash)\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestFindStanza_Absent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `export FOO=bar\n`\n\tversion, body, found := FindStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif found {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected stanza to not be found\")\n\t}\n\tif version != -1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"version = %d, want -1\", version)\n\t}\n\tif body != \"\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"body = %q, want empty\", body)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestFindStanza_DifferentName(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN other-tool (v1)\nsomething\n# END other-tool\n`\n\t_, _, found := FindStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif found {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"should not find stanza with different name\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestFindStanza_MultilineBody(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\nline1\nline2\nline3\n# END my-tool\n`\n\tversion, body, found := FindStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif !found {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected stanza to be found\")\n\t}\n\tif version != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"version = %d, want 1\", version)\n\t}\n\tif body != \"line1\\nline2\\nline3\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"body = %q, want %q\", body, \"line1\\nline2\\nline3\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_EmptyContent(t *testing.T) {\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(\"\", \"my-tool\", 1, \"source <(my-tool completion bash)\")\n\twant := \"# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\\nsource <(my-tool completion bash)\\n# END my-tool\\n\"\n\tif result != want {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"result = %q, want %q\", result, want)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_AppendToExistingContent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := \"export FOO=bar\\n\"\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(content, \"my-tool\", 1, \"source <(my-tool completion bash)\")\n\twant := \"export FOO=bar\\n\\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\\nsource <(my-tool completion bash)\\n# END my-tool\\n\"\n\tif result != want {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"result = %q, want %q\", result, want)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_ReplaceExisting(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `export FOO=bar\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\nold-command\n# END my-tool\n\nexport BAZ=qux\n`\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(content, \"my-tool\", 2, \"new-command\")\n\twant := `export FOO=bar\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v2)\nnew-command\n# END my-tool\n\nexport BAZ=qux\n`\n\tif result != want {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"result = %q, want %q\", result, want)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_PreservesSurroundingContent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# header\nexport A=1\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\nold-line\n# END my-tool\n\n# footer\nexport B=2\n`\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(content, \"my-tool\", 2, \"new-line\")\n\n\t// Check surrounding content is preserved\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"# header\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"header should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export A=1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"content before stanza should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"# footer\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"footer should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export B=2\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"content after stanza should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_NoTrailingNewline(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := \"export FOO=bar\"\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(content, \"my-tool\", 1, \"cmd\")\n\twant := \"export FOO=bar\\n\\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\\ncmd\\n# END my-tool\\n\"\n\tif result != want {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"result = %q, want %q\", result, want)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestUpsertStanza_MultipleStanzas(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN tool-a (v1)\ncmd-a\n# END tool-a\n`\n\tresult := UpsertStanza(content, \"tool-b\", 1, \"cmd-b\")\n\t// tool-a should remain, tool-b appended\n\t_, _, foundA := FindStanza(result, \"tool-a\")\n\tif !foundA {\n\t\tt.Error(\"tool-a stanza should still exist\")\n\t}\n\t_, _, foundB := FindStanza(result, \"tool-b\")\n\tif !foundB {\n\t\tt.Error(\"tool-b stanza should be appended\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_Present(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `export FOO=bar\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\nsource <(my-tool completion bash)\n# END my-tool\n\nexport BAZ=qux\n`\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif containsLine(result, \"# BEGIN my-tool\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza BEGIN marker should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif containsLine(result, \"# END my-tool\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza END marker should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif containsLine(result, \"my-tool completion\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza body should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export FOO=bar\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"content before stanza should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export BAZ=qux\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"content after stanza should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_Absent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := \"export FOO=bar\\n\"\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif result != content {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"content should be unchanged, got %q\", result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_AtBeginning(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\ncmd\n# END my-tool\n\nexport FOO=bar\n`\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif containsLine(result, \"# BEGIN my-tool\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export FOO=bar\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"remaining content should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_AtEnd(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `export FOO=bar\n\n# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\ncmd\n# END my-tool\n`\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\tif containsLine(result, \"# BEGIN my-tool\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsLine(result, \"export FOO=bar\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"remaining content should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_OnlyStanza(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN my-tool (v1)\ncmd\n# END my-tool\n`\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"my-tool\")\n\t// Should result in empty or whitespace-only content\n\tif containsLine(result, \"# BEGIN my-tool\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza should be removed\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveStanza_PreservesOtherStanzas(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := `# BEGIN tool-a (v1)\ncmd-a\n# END tool-a\n\n# BEGIN tool-b (v1)\ncmd-b\n# END tool-b\n`\n\tresult := RemoveStanza(content, \"tool-a\")\n\t_, _, foundA := FindStanza(result, \"tool-a\")\n\tif foundA {\n\t\tt.Error(\"tool-a should be removed\")\n\t}\n\t_, _, foundB := FindStanza(result, \"tool-b\")\n\tif !foundB {\n\t\tt.Error(\"tool-b should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc containsLine(content, line string) bool {\n\tfor _, l := range splitLines(content) {\n\t\tif l == line {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc splitLines(s string) []string {\n\tlines := make([]string, 0)\n\tfor _, line := range split(s, \"\\n\") {\n\t\tlines = append(lines, line)\n\t}\n\treturn lines\n}\n\nfunc split(s, sep string) []string {\n\tif s == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tresult := make([]string, 0)\n\tfor {\n\t\tidx := indexOf(s, sep)\n\t\tif idx < 0 {\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, s)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult = append(result, s[:idx])\n\t\ts = s[idx+len(sep):]\n\t}\n\treturn result\n}\n\nfunc indexOf(s, sub string) int {\n\tfor i := range len(s) - len(sub) + 1 {\n\t\tif s[i:i+len(sub)] == sub {\n\t\t\treturn i\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn -1\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/paul/src/entireio/completion-stanza-manager/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go",
  "old_string": "// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.\nfunc isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {\n\t//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input\n\tcontent, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Contains(string(content), \"entire completion\")\n}",
  "new_string": "// completionStatus checks the state of shell completion in the rc file.\nfunc completionStatus(rcFile string) completionStatusKind {\n\t//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn statusMissing\n\t}\n\tcontent := string(data)\n\n\t// Check for stanza format first\n\tversion, _, found := FindStanza(content, stanzaName)\n\tif found {\n\t\tif version >= stanzaVersion {\n\t\t\treturn statusCurrent\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn statusStale\n\t}\n\n\t// Check for legacy format (comment + completion line without stanza markers)\n\tif strings.Contains(content, legacyCompletionComment) && strings.Contains(content, \"entire completion\") {\n\t\treturn statusLegacy\n\t}\n\n\treturn statusMissing\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/paul/src/entireio/completion-stanza-manager/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go",
  "old_string": "// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.\nfunc appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"creating directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\t//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability\n\tf, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"opening file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer f.Close()\n\n\t_, err = f.WriteString(\"\\n\" + shellCompletionComment + \"\\n\" + completionLine + \"\\n\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"writing completion: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// installShellCompletion adds or updates the completion stanza in the rc file.\n// If legacy format is present, it is migrated to the stanza format.\nfunc installShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"creating directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"reading file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tcontent := string(data)\n\n\t// Remove legacy format if present\n\tcontent = removeLegacyCompletion(content)\n\n\t// Upsert the stanza\n\tcontent = UpsertStanza(content, stanzaName, stanzaVersion, completionLine)\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"writing file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// removeShellCompletion removes the completion stanza and any legacy format from the rc file.\n// Returns true if the file was modified.\nfunc removeShellCompletion(rcFile string) (bool, error) {\n\t//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn false, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"reading file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\toriginal := string(data)\n\tcontent := original\n\n\t// Remove stanza format\n\tcontent = RemoveStanza(content, stanzaName)\n\n\t// Remove legacy format\n\tcontent = removeLegacyCompletion(content)\n\n\tif content == original {\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:gosec // G306: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, fmt.Errorf(\"writing file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn true, nil\n}\n\n// removeLegacyCompletion removes the old non-stanza format\n// (\"# Entire CLI shell completion\" comment + following completion line).\nfunc removeLegacyCompletion(content string) string {\n\tif !strings.Contains(content, legacyCompletionComment) {\n\t\treturn content\n\t}\n\n\tlines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\tvar result []string\n\ti := 0\n\tfor i < len(lines) {\n\t\tif strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]) == legacyCompletionComment {\n\t\t\t// Skip the comment line\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t// Skip the completion line that follows if it contains \"entire completion\"\n\t\t\tif i < len(lines) && strings.Contains(lines[i], \"entire completion\") {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Skip a trailing blank line\n\t\t\tif i < len(lines) && strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]) == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult = append(result, lines[i])\n\t\ti++\n\t}\n\n\toutput := strings.Join(result, \"\\n\")\n\toutput = strings.TrimRight(output, \"\\n\")\n\tif output != \"\" {\n\t\toutput += \"\\n\"\n\t}\n\treturn output\n}"
}

(detected: %s)\", shellName)).\n\t\t\t\tOptions(\n\t\t\t\t\thuh.NewOption(\"Yes\", \"yes\"),\n\t\t\t\t\thuh.NewOption(\"No\", \"no\"),\n\t\t\t\t).\n\t\t\t\tValue(&selected),\n\t\t),\n\t)\n\n\tif err := form.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif selected != \"yes\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update %s: %w\", rcFile, err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"✓ Shell completion added to %s\\n\", rcFile)\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  Restart your shell to activate\")",
  "new_string": "\tstatus := completionStatus(rcFile)\n\tif status == statusCurrent {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\\n\", rcFile)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar promptTitle string\n\tswitch status {\n\tcase statusStale:\n\t\tpromptTitle = fmt.Sprintf(\"Update shell completion? (detected: %s, newer version available)\", shellName)\n\tcase statusLegacy:\n\t\tpromptTitle = fmt.Sprintf(\"Migrate shell completion to managed format? (detected: %s)\", shellName)\n\tdefault:\n\t\tpromptTitle = fmt.Sprintf(\"Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)\", shellName)\n\t}\n\n\tvar selected string\n\tform := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\thuh.NewSelect[string]().\n\t\t\t\tTitle(promptTitle).\n\t\t\t\tOptions(\n\t\t\t\t\thuh.NewOption(\"Yes\", \"yes\"),\n\t\t\t\t\thuh.NewOption(\"No\", \"no\"),\n\t\t\t\t).\n\t\t\t\tValue(&selected),\n\t\t),\n\t)\n\n\tif err := form.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif selected != \"yes\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif err := installShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update %s: %w\", rcFile, err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"✓ Shell completion added to %s\\n\", rcFile)\n\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"  Restart your shell to activate\")"

   951→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   952→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   953→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   954→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   955→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   956→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	// Gather counts for display
   960→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   961→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   962→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   963→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   964→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   965→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   966→
   967→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   968→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   969→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
   970→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   971→		return nil
   972→	}
   973→

   979→		}

   981→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")

   984→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)

    19→// setupTestDir creates a temp directory, changes to it, and returns it.
    20→// It also registers cleanup to restore the original directory.
    21→func setupTestDir(t *testing.T) string {
    22→	t.Helper()
    23→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
    24→	t.Chdir(tmpDir)
    25→	paths.ClearRepoRootCache()
    26→	return tmpDir
    27→}
    28→
    29→// setupTestRepo creates a temp directory with a git repo initialized.
    30→func setupTestRepo(t *testing.T) {
    31→	t.Helper()
    32→	tmpDir := setupTestDir(t)
    33→	if _, err := git.PlainInit(tmpDir, false); err != nil {
    34→		t.Fatalf("Failed to init repo: %v", err)
    35→	}
    36→}

   342→func TestRunEnableWithStrategy_PreservesExistingSettings(t *testing.T) {
   343→	setupTestRepo(t)
   344→
   345→	// Create initial settings with strategy_options (like push enabled)
   346→	initialSettings := `{
   347→		"strategy": "manual-commit",
   348→		"enabled": true,
   349→		"strategy_options": {
   350→			"push": true,
   351→			"some_other_option": "value"
   352→		}
   353→	}`
   354→	writeSettings(t, initialSettings)
   355→
   356→	// Run enable with a different strategy
   357→	var stdout bytes.Buffer
   358→	err := runEnableWithStrategy(&stdout, "auto-commit", false, false, false, true, false, false, false)
   359→	if err != nil {
   360→		t.Fatalf("runEnableWithStrategy() error = %v", err)
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Load the saved settings and verify strategy_options were preserved
   364→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		t.Fatalf("LoadEntireSettings() error = %v", err)
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Strategy should be updated
   370→	if settings.Strategy != "auto-commit" {
   371→		t.Errorf("Strategy should be 'auto-commit', got %q", settings.Strategy)
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// strategy_options should be preserved
   375→	if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   376→		t.Fatal("strategy_options should be preserved, but got nil")
   377→	}
   378→	if settings.StrategyOptions["push"] != true {
   379→		t.Errorf("strategy_options.push should be true, got %v", settings.StrategyOptions["push"])
   380→	}
   381→	if settings.StrategyOptions["some_other_option"] != "value" {
   382→		t.Errorf("strategy_options.some_other_option should be 'value', got %v", settings.StrategyOptions["some_other_option"])
   383→	}
   384→}

   389→	// Create project settings
   390→	writeSettings(t, `{"strategy": "manual-commit", "enabled": true}`)
   391→
   392→	// Create local settings with strategy_options
   393→	localSettings := `{
   394→		"strategy_options": {
   395→			"push": true
   396→		}
   397→	}`
   398→	writeLocalSettings(t, localSettings)
   399→
   400→	// Run enable with --local flag
   401→	var stdout bytes.Buffer
   402→	err := runEnableWithStrategy(&stdout, "auto-commit", false, false, true, false, false, false, false)
   403→	if err != nil {
   404→		t.Fatalf("runEnableWithStrategy() error = %v", err)
   405→	}
   406→
   407→	// Load the merged settings (project + local)
   408→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   409→	if err != nil {
   410→		t.Fatalf("LoadEntireSettings() error = %v", err)
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	// Strategy should be updated (from local)
   414→	if settings.Strategy != "auto-commit" {
   415→		t.Errorf("Strategy should be 'auto-commit', got %q", settings.Strategy)
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	// strategy_options.push should be preserved
   419→	if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   420→		t.Fatal("strategy_options should be preserved, but got nil")
   421→	}
   422→	if settings.StrategyOptions["push"] != true {
   423→		t.Errorf("strategy_options.push should be true, got %v", settings.StrategyOptions["push"])
   424→	}
   425→}

   589→func TestShellCompletionTarget(t *testing.T) {
   590→	tests := []struct {
   591→		name             string
   592→		shell            string
   593→		createBashProf   bool
   594→		wantShell        string
   595→		wantRCBase       string // basename of rc file
   596→		wantCompletion   string
   597→		wantErrUnsupport bool
   598→	}{
   599→		{
   600→			name:           "zsh",
   601→			shell:          "/bin/zsh",
   602→			wantShell:      "Zsh",
   603→			wantRCBase:     ".zshrc",
   604→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion zsh)",
   605→		},
   606→		{
   607→			name:           "bash_no_profile",
   608→			shell:          "/bin/bash",
   609→			wantShell:      "Bash",
   610→			wantRCBase:     ".bashrc",
   611→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion bash)",
   612→		},
   613→		{
   614→			name:           "bash_with_profile",
   615→			shell:          "/bin/bash",
   616→			createBashProf: true,
   617→			wantShell:      "Bash",
   618→			wantRCBase:     ".bash_profile",
   619→			wantCompletion: "source <(entire completion bash)",
   620→		},
   621→		{
   622→			name:           "fish",
   623→			shell:          "/usr/bin/fish",
   624→			wantShell:      "Fish",
   625→			wantRCBase:     filepath.Join(".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   626→			wantCompletion: "entire completion fish | source",
   627→		},
   628→		{
   629→			name:             "empty_shell",
   630→			shell:            "",
   631→			wantErrUnsupport: true,
   632→		},
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	for _, tt := range tests {
   636→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   637→			home := t.TempDir()
   638→			t.Setenv("HOME", home)
   639→			t.Setenv("SHELL", tt.shell)
   640→
   641→			if tt.createBashProf {
   642→				if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile"), []byte(""), 0o644); err != nil {
   643→					t.Fatal(err)
   644→				}
   645→			}
   646→
   647→			shellName, rcFile, completion, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   648→
   649→			if tt.wantErrUnsupport {
   650→				if err != errUnsupportedShell {
   651→					t.Fatalf("got err=%v, want errUnsupportedShell", err)
   652→				}
   653→				return
   654→			}
   655→			if err != nil {
   656→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   657→			}
   658→			if shellName != tt.wantShell {
   659→				t.Errorf("shellName = %q, want %q", shellName, tt.wantShell)
   660→			}
   661→			wantRC := filepath.Join(home, tt.wantRCBase)
   662→			if rcFile != wantRC {
   663→				t.Errorf("rcFile = %q, want %q", rcFile, wantRC)
   664→			}
   665→			if completion != tt.wantCompletion {
   666→				t.Errorf("completion = %q, want %q", completion, tt.wantCompletion)
   667→			}
   668→		})
   669→	}
   670→}
   671→
   672→func TestAppendShellCompletion(t *testing.T) {
   673→	tests := []struct {
   674→		name           string
   675→		rcFileRelPath  string
   676→		completionLine string
   677→		preExisting    string // existing content in rc file; empty means file doesn't exist
   678→		createParent   bool   // whether parent dir already exists
   679→	}{
   680→		{
   681→			name:           "zsh_new_file",
   682→			rcFileRelPath:  ".zshrc",
   683→			completionLine: "source <(entire completion zsh)",
   684→			createParent:   true,
   685→		},
   686→		{
   687→			name:           "zsh_existing_file",
   688→			rcFileRelPath:  ".zshrc",
   689→			completionLine: "source <(entire completion zsh)",
   690→			preExisting:    "# existing zshrc content\n",
   691→			createParent:   true,
   692→		},
   693→		{
   694→			name:           "fish_no_parent_dir",
   695→			rcFileRelPath:  filepath.Join(".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   696→			completionLine: "entire completion fish | source",
   697→			createParent:   false,
   698→		},
   699→		{
   700→			name:           "fish_existing_dir",
   701→			rcFileRelPath:  filepath.Join(".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   702→			completionLine: "entire completion fish | source",
   703→			createParent:   true,
   704→		},
   705→	}
   706→
   707→	for _, tt := range tests {
   708→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   709→			home := t.TempDir()
   710→			rcFile := filepath.Join(home, tt.rcFileRelPath)
   711→
   712→			if tt.createParent {
   713→				if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o755); err != nil {
   714→					t.Fatal(err)
   715→				}
   716→			}
   717→			if tt.preExisting != "" {
   718→				if err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(tt.preExisting), 0o644); err != nil {
   719→					t.Fatal(err)
   720→				}
   721→			}
   722→
   723→			if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, tt.completionLine); err != nil {
   724→				t.Fatalf("appendShellCompletion() error: %v", err)
   725→			}
   726→
   727→			// Verify the file was created and contains the completion line.
   728→			data, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   729→			if err != nil {
   730→				t.Fatalf("reading rc file: %v", err)
   731→			}
   732→			content := string(data)
   733→
   734→			if !strings.Contains(content, shellCompletionComment) {
   735→				t.Errorf("rc file missing comment %q", shellCompletionComment)
   736→			}
   737→			if !strings.Contains(content, tt.completionLine) {
   738→				t.Errorf("rc file missing completion line %q", tt.completionLine)
   739→			}
   740→			if tt.preExisting != "" && !strings.HasPrefix(content, tt.preExisting) {
   741→				t.Errorf("pre-existing content was overwritten")
   742→			}
   743→
   744→			// Verify parent directory permissions.
   745→			info, err := os.Stat(filepath.Dir(rcFile))
   746→			if err != nil {
   747→				t.Fatalf("stat parent dir: %v", err)
   748→			}
   749→			if !info.IsDir() {
   750→				t.Fatal("parent path is not a directory")
   751→			}
   752→		})
   753→	}
   754→}
