    39→func newEnableCmd() *cobra.Command {
    40→	var localDev bool
    41→	var ignoreUntracked bool
    42→	var useLocalSettings bool
    43→	var useProjectSettings bool
    44→	var agentName string
    45→	var strategyFlag string
    46→	var forceHooks bool
    47→	var skipPushSessions bool
    48→	var telemetry bool
    49→
    50→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:   "enable",
    52→		Short: "Enable Entire",
    53→		Long: `Enable Entire with session tracking for your AI agent workflows.
    54→
    55→Uses the manual-commit strategy by default. To use a different strategy:
    56→
    57→  entire enable --strategy auto-commit
    58→
    59→Strategies: manual-commit (default), auto-commit`,
    60→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    61→			// Check if we're in a git repository first - this is a prerequisite error,
    62→			// not a usage error, so we silence Cobra's output and use SilentError
    63→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go
    64→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    65→				cmd.SilenceUsage = true
    66→				cmd.SilenceErrors = true
    67→				fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run 'entire enable' from within a git repository.")
    68→				return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
    69→			}
    70→
    71→			if err := validateSetupFlags(useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings); err != nil {
    72→				return err
    73→			}
    74→			// Non-interactive mode if --agent flag is provided
    75→			if agentName != "" {
    76→				return setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(agent.AgentName(agentName), strategyFlag, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
    77→			}
    78→			// If strategy is specified via flag, skip interactive selection
    79→			if strategyFlag != "" {
    80→				return runEnableWithStrategy(cmd.OutOrStdout(), strategyFlag, localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
    81→			}
    82→			return runEnableInteractive(cmd.OutOrStdout(), localDev, ignoreUntracked, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry)
    83→		},
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&localDev, "local-dev", false, "Use go run instead of entire binary for hooks")
    87→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("local-dev") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
    88→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&ignoreUntracked, "ignore-untracked", false, "Commit all new files without tracking pre-existing untracked files")
    89→	cmd.Flags().MarkHidden("ignore-untracked") //nolint:errcheck,gosec // flag is defined above
    90→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useLocalSettings, "local", false, "Write settings to settings.local.json instead of settings.json")
    91→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Write settings to settings.json even if it already exists")
    92→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&agentName, "agent", "", "Agent to setup hooks for (e.g., claude-code). Enables non-interactive mode.")
    93→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&strategyFlag, "strategy", "", "Strategy to use (manual-commit or auto-commit)")
    94→	cmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&forceHooks, "force", "f", false, "Force reinstall hooks (removes existing Entire hooks first)")
    95→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&skipPushSessions, "skip-push-sessions", false, "Disable automatic pushing of session logs on git push")
    96→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&telemetry, "telemetry", true, "Enable anonymous usage analytics")
    97→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // completion is optional, flag is defined above
    98→	cmd.RegisterFlagCompletionFunc("strategy", func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string, _ string) ([]string, cobra.ShellCompDirective) {
    99→		return []string{strategyDisplayManualCommit, strategyDisplayAutoCommit}, cobra.ShellCompDirectiveNoFileComp
   100→	})
   101→
   102→	// Add subcommands for automation/testing
   103→	cmd.AddCommand(newSetupGitHookCmd())
   104→
   105→	return cmd
   106→}

   108→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   109→	var useProjectSettings bool
   110→	var uninstall bool
   111→	var force bool
   112→
   113→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   114→		Use:   "disable",
   115→		Short: "Disable Entire",
   116→		Long: `Disable Entire temporarily. Hooks will exit silently and commands will show a disabled message.
   117→
   118→Use --uninstall to completely remove Entire from this repository, including:
   119→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   120→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   121→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   122→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   123→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,
   124→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   125→			if uninstall {
   126→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   127→			}
   128→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   129→		},
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&useProjectSettings, "project", false, "Update settings.json instead of settings.local.json")
   133→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&uninstall, "uninstall", false, "Completely remove Entire from this repository")
   134→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")
   135→
   136→	return cmd
   137→}
   138→
   139→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   140→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   141→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   142→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, selectedStrategy string, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   143→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   144→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   145→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   146→		internalStrategy = mapped
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Validate the strategy exists
   150→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   156→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   157→	if err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   161→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   162→	} else {
   163→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Setup .entire directory
   167→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→	if dirCreated {
   172→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   176→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   179→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   180→	}
   181→	// Update the specific fields
   182→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   183→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   184→	settings.Enabled = true
   185→
   186→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   187→	if skipPushSessions {
   188→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   189→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   190→		}
   191→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   195→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   196→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   197→		f := false
   198→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   202→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   203→	if err != nil {
   204→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   205→	}
   206→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   207→
   208→	if showNotification {
   209→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   210→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	if shouldUseLocal {
   214→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   215→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   216→		}
   217→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   218→	} else {
   219→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   220→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   221→		}
   222→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   226→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   227→	if err != nil {
   228→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   229→	}
   230→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   231→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   232→	} else {
   233→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   237→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   238→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Show success message with display name
   242→	displayName := selectedStrategy
   243→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy]; ok {
   244→		displayName = dn
   245→	}
   246→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", displayName)
   247→
   248→	return nil
   249→}
   250→
   251→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   252→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   253→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   254→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   255→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Using %s strategy (use --strategy to change)\n\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   256→
   257→	// Setup Claude Code hooks
   258→	hooksInstalled, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks)
   259→	if err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	if hooksInstalled > 0 {
   263→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks installed")
   264→	} else {
   265→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Claude Code hooks verified")
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Setup .entire directory
   269→	dirCreated, err := setupEntireDirectory()
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   272→	}
   273→	if dirCreated {
   274→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ .entire directory created")
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   278→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   279→	if err != nil {
   280→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   281→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   282→	}
   283→	// Update the specific fields
   284→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   285→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   286→	settings.Enabled = true
   287→
   288→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   289→	if skipPushSessions {
   290→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   291→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   292→		}
   293→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   297→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   298→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Determine which settings file to write to (interactive prompt if settings.json exists)
   302→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   305→	}
   306→	shouldUseLocal, err := promptSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return err
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	if shouldUseLocal {
   312→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   313→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   314→		}
   315→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Local settings saved (.entire/settings.local.json)")
   316→	} else {
   317→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   318→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   319→		}
   320→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Project settings saved (.entire/settings.json)")
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   324→	gitHooksInstalled, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true)
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   327→	}
   328→	if gitHooksInstalled > 0 {
   329→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks installed")
   330→	} else {
   331→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Git hooks verified")
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   335→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   336→	if err != nil {
   337→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   338→	}
   339→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   340→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Show success message with display name
   344→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n✓ %s strategy enabled\n", strategyInternalToDisplay[internalStrategy])
   345→
   346→	return nil
   347→}
   348→
   349→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   350→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   351→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	settings.Enabled = true
   357→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   358→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   362→	return nil
   363→}
   364→
   365→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   366→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   367→	if err != nil {
   368→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	settings.Enabled = false
   372→
   373→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   374→	if useProjectSettings {
   375→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   376→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   377→		}
   378→	} else {
   379→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   380→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   381→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   382→		}
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   386→	return nil
   387→}
   388→
   389→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   390→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   391→
   392→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   393→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   394→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   395→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   396→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		// Default to enabled on error
   399→		return false
   400→	}
   401→	if !enabled {
   402→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   403→		return true
   404→	}
   405→	return false
   406→}
   407→
   408→// setupClaudeCodeHook sets up Claude Code hooks.
   409→// This is a convenience wrapper that uses the agent package.
   410→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   411→func setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) {
   412→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get claude-code agent: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   418→	if !ok {
   419→		return 0, errors.New("claude-code agent does not support hooks")
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   423→	if err != nil {
   424→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install claude-code hooks: %w", err)
   425→	}
   426→
   427→	return count, nil
   428→}
   429→
   430→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   431→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   432→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(agentName agent.AgentName, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   433→	ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
   434→	if err != nil {
   435→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", agentName)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   439→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   440→	if !ok {
   441→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Install hooks
   445→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	if count == 0 {
   451→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   452→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   453→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   454→		}
   455→		fmt.Println(msg)
   456→	} else {
   457→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", count, ag.Description())
   458→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   459→			msg += " - This is a work in progress"
   460→		}
   461→		fmt.Println(msg)
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	// Update settings to store the strategy
   465→	settings, _ := LoadEntireSettings() //nolint:errcheck // settings defaults are fine
   466→	settings.Enabled = true
   467→	if localDev {
   468→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   472→	if skipPushSessions {
   473→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   474→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   475→		}
   476→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Set strategy if provided
   480→	if strategyName != "" {
   481→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   482→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   483→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   484→			internalStrategy = mapped
   485→		}
   486→		// Validate the strategy exists
   487→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   488→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   489→		}
   490→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   494→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   495→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   496→		f := false
   497→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   501→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	// Install git hooks (always reinstall to ensure they're up-to-date)
   505→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   506→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/sessions branch, etc.)
   510→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   511→	if err != nil {
   512→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   513→	}
   514→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   515→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	return nil
   519→}
   520→
   521→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   522→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   523→	if useLocal && useProject {
   524→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   525→	}
   526→	return nil
   527→}
   528→
   529→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   530→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   531→// - The --local and --project flags
   532→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   533→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   534→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   535→	if useLocal {
   536→		return true, false
   537→	}
   538→
   539→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   540→	if useProject {
   541→		return false, false
   542→	}
   543→
   544→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   545→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   546→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   547→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   548→		return true, true
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   552→	return false, false
   553→}
   554→
   555→// Settings target options for interactive prompt
   556→const (
   557→	settingsTargetProject = "project"
   558→	settingsTargetLocal   = "local"
   559→)
   560→
   561→// promptSettingsTarget interactively asks the user where to save settings
   562→// when settings.json already exists and no flags were provided.
   563→// Returns (useLocal, error).
   564→func promptSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, error) {
   565→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   566→	if useLocal {
   567→		return true, nil
   568→	}
   569→
   570→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   571→	if useProject {
   572→		return false, nil
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// Check if settings file exists
   576→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   577→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   578→		// Settings file doesn't exist - create it (no prompt needed)
   579→		return false, nil
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Settings file exists - prompt user
   583→	var selected string
   584→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   585→		huh.NewGroup(
   586→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   587→				Title("Project settings already exist. Where should settings be saved?").
   588→				Options(
   589→					huh.NewOption("Update project settings (settings.json)", settingsTargetProject),
   590→					huh.NewOption("Use local settings (settings.local.json, gitignored)", settingsTargetLocal),
   591→				).
   592→				Value(&selected),
   593→		),
   594→	)
   595→
   596→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   597→		return false, fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   598→	}
   599→
   600→	return selected == settingsTargetLocal, nil
   601→}
   602→
   603→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   604→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   605→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) {
   606→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   607→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   608→	if err != nil {
   609→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   610→	}
   611→
   612→	// Check if directory already exists
   613→	created := false
   614→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   615→		created = true
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Create .entire directory
   619→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   620→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   621→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   625→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   626→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	return created, nil
   630→}
   631→
   632→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   633→func setupGitHook() error {
   634→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   635→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   636→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   637→	if err != nil {
   638→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   639→	}
   640→	return nil
   641→}
   642→
   643→// newSetupGitHookCmd creates the standalone git-hook setup command
   644→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {
   645→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   646→		Use:    "git-hook",
   647→		Short:  "Install git hook for session context trailers",
   648→		Hidden: true, // Hidden as it's mainly for testing
   649→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   650→			return setupGitHook()
   651→		},
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	return cmd
   655→}
   656→
   657→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   658→	return &cobra.Command{
   659→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   660→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   661→		Hidden: true,
   662→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   663→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   664→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   665→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   666→			}
   667→			return nil
   668→		},
   669→	}
   670→}
   671→
   672→// Stanza constants for shell completion management.
   673→const (
   674→	stanzaName    = "entire-cli"
   675→	stanzaVersion = 1
   676→)
   677→
   678→// completionStatusKind represents the state of shell completion in an rc file.
   679→type completionStatusKind int
   680→
   681→const (
   682→	statusCurrent completionStatusKind = iota // stanza exists at current version
   683→	statusStale                               // stanza exists at older version
   684→	statusLegacy                              // old non-stanza format detected
   685→	statusMissing                             // nothing found
   686→)
   687→
   688→// legacyCompletionComment is the comment from the old non-stanza format.
   689→const legacyCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   690→
   691→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   692→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   693→
   694→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   695→// user's current shell.
   696→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   697→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   698→	if err != nil {
   699→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   700→	}
   701→
   702→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   703→	switch {
   704→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   705→		return "Zsh",
   706→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   707→			"source <(entire completion zsh)",
   708→			nil
   709→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   710→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   711→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   712→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   713→		}
   714→		return "Bash",
   715→			bashRC,
   716→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   717→			nil
   718→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   719→		return "Fish",
   720→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   721→			"entire completion fish | source",
   722→			nil
   723→	default:
   724→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   725→	}
   726→}
   727→
   728→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   729→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   730→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   731→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   732→	if err != nil {
   733→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   734→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   735→			return nil
   736→		}
   737→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   738→	}
   739→
   740→	status := completionStatus(rcFile)
   741→	if status == statusCurrent {
   742→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   743→		return nil
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	var promptTitle string
   747→	switch status {
   748→	case statusStale:
   749→		promptTitle = fmt.Sprintf("Update shell completion? (detected: %s, newer version available)", shellName)
   750→	case statusLegacy:
   751→		promptTitle = fmt.Sprintf("Migrate shell completion to managed format? (detected: %s)", shellName)
   752→	default:
   753→		promptTitle = fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	var selected string
   757→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   758→		huh.NewGroup(
   759→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   760→				Title(promptTitle).
   761→				Options(
   762→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   763→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   764→				).
   765→				Value(&selected),
   766→		),
   767→	)
   768→
   769→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   770→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   771→		return nil
   772→	}
   773→
   774→	if selected != "yes" {
   775→		return nil
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	if err := installShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   779→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   783→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   784→
   785→	return nil
   786→}
   787→
   788→// completionStatus checks the state of shell completion in the rc file.
   789→func completionStatus(rcFile string) completionStatusKind {
   790→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   791→	data, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		return statusMissing
   794→	}
   795→	content := string(data)
   796→
   797→	// Check for stanza format first
   798→	version, _, found := FindStanza(content, stanzaName)
   799→	if found {
   800→		if version >= stanzaVersion {
   801→			return statusCurrent
   802→		}
   803→		return statusStale
   804→	}
   805→
   806→	// Check for legacy format (comment + completion line without stanza markers)
   807→	if strings.Contains(content, legacyCompletionComment) && strings.Contains(content, "entire completion") {
   808→		return statusLegacy
   809→	}
   810→
   811→	return statusMissing
   812→}
   813→
   814→// installShellCompletion adds or updates the completion stanza in the rc file.
   815→// If legacy format is present, it is migrated to the stanza format.
   816→func installShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   817→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
   818→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
   819→	}
   820→
   821→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   822→	data, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   823→	if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   824→		return fmt.Errorf("reading file: %w", err)
   825→	}
   826→	content := string(data)
   827→
   828→	// Remove legacy format if present
   829→	content = removeLegacyCompletion(content)
   830→
   831→	// Upsert the stanza
   832→	content = UpsertStanza(content, stanzaName, stanzaVersion, completionLine)
   833→
   834→	//nolint:gosec // G306: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   835→	if err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   836→		return fmt.Errorf("writing file: %w", err)
   837→	}
   838→	return nil
   839→}
   840→
   841→// removeShellCompletion removes the completion stanza and any legacy format from the rc file.
   842→// Returns true if the file was modified.
   843→func removeShellCompletion(rcFile string) (bool, error) {
   844→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   845→	data, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   846→	if err != nil {
   847→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   848→			return false, nil
   849→		}
   850→		return false, fmt.Errorf("reading file: %w", err)
   851→	}
   852→	original := string(data)
   853→	content := original
   854→
   855→	// Remove stanza format
   856→	content = RemoveStanza(content, stanzaName)
   857→
   858→	// Remove legacy format
   859→	content = removeLegacyCompletion(content)
   860→
   861→	if content == original {
   862→		return false, nil
   863→	}
   864→
   865→	//nolint:gosec // G306: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   866→	if err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   867→		return false, fmt.Errorf("writing file: %w", err)
   868→	}
   869→	return true, nil
   870→}
   871→
   872→// removeLegacyCompletion removes the old non-stanza format
   873→// ("# Entire CLI shell completion" comment + following completion line).
   874→func removeLegacyCompletion(content string) string {
   875→	if !strings.Contains(content, legacyCompletionComment) {
   876→		return content
   877→	}
   878→
   879→	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   880→	var result []string
   881→	i := 0
   882→	for i < len(lines) {
   883→		if strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]) == legacyCompletionComment {
   884→			// Skip the comment line
   885→			i++
   886→			// Skip the completion line that follows if it contains "entire completion"
   887→			if i < len(lines) && strings.Contains(lines[i], "entire completion") {
   888→				i++
   889→			}
   890→			// Skip a trailing blank line
   891→			if i < len(lines) && strings.TrimSpace(lines[i]) == "" {
   892→				i++
   893→			}
   894→			continue
   895→		}
   896→		result = append(result, lines[i])
   897→		i++
   898→	}
   899→
   900→	output := strings.Join(result, "\n")
   901→	output = strings.TrimRight(output, "\n")
   902→	if output != "" {
   903→		output += "\n"
   904→	}
   905→	return output
   906→}
   907→
   908→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   909→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   910→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   911→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   912→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   913→	if !telemetryFlag {
   914→		f := false
   915→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   916→		return nil
   917→	}
   918→
   919→	// Skip if already asked
   920→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   921→		return nil
   922→	}
   923→
   924→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   925→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   926→		f := false
   927→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   928→		return nil
   929→	}
   930→
   931→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   932→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   933→		huh.NewGroup(
   934→			huh.NewConfirm().
   935→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   936→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   937→				Affirmative("Yes").
   938→				Negative("No").
   939→				Value(&consent),
   940→		),
   941→	)
   942→
   943→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   944→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
   945→	}
   946→
   947→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
   948→	return nil
   949→}
   950→
   951→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   952→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   953→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   954→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   955→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   956→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	// Gather counts for display
   960→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   961→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   962→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   963→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   964→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   965→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   966→
   967→	// Check shell completion status
   968→	_, completionRCFile, _, shellErr := shellCompletionTarget()
   969→	hasShellCompletion := false
   970→	if shellErr == nil {
   971→		status := completionStatus(completionRCFile)
   972→		hasShellCompletion = status != statusMissing
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   976→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   977→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled && !hasShellCompletion {
   978→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   979→		return nil
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
   983→	if !force {
   984→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
   985→		if entireDirExists {
   986→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
   987→		}
   988→		if gitHooksInstalled {
   989→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   990→		}
   991→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
   992→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
   993→		}
   994→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
   995→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
   996→		}
   997→		switch {
   998→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
   999→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
  1000→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
  1001→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
  1002→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
  1003→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
  1004→		}
  1005→		if hasShellCompletion {
  1006→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shell completion (%s)\n", completionRCFile)
  1007→		}
  1008→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
  1009→
  1010→		var confirmed bool
  1011→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
  1012→			huh.NewGroup(
  1013→				huh.NewConfirm().
  1014→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
  1015→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
  1016→					Negative("Cancel").
  1017→					Value(&confirmed),
  1018→			),
  1019→		)
  1020→
  1021→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
  1022→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1023→		}
  1024→
  1025→		if !confirmed {
  1026→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
  1027→			return nil
  1028→		}
  1029→	}
  1030→
  1031→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
  1032→
  1033→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
  1034→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
  1035→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
  1036→	}
  1037→
  1038→	// 2. Remove git hooks
  1039→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
  1040→	if err != nil {
  1041→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
  1042→	} else if removed > 0 {
  1043→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
  1044→	}
  1045→
  1046→	// 3. Remove session state files
  1047→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
  1048→	if err != nil {
  1049→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
  1050→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
  1051→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
  1052→	}
  1053→
  1054→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
  1055→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
  1056→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
  1057→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1058→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1059→	}
  1060→
  1061→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1062→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1063→	if err != nil {
  1064→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1065→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1066→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	// 6. Remove shell completion from rc file
  1070→	if hasShellCompletion {
  1071→		modified, removeErr := removeShellCompletion(completionRCFile)
  1072→		if removeErr != nil {
  1073→			fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shell completion: %v\n", removeErr)
  1074→		} else if modified {
  1075→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed shell completion from %s\n", completionRCFile)
  1076→		}
  1077→	}
  1078→
  1079→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1080→	return nil
  1081→}

  1083→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1084→func countSessionStates() int {
  1085→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1086→	if err != nil {
  1087→		return 0
  1088→	}
  1089→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1090→	if err != nil {
  1091→		return 0
  1092→	}
  1093→	return len(states)
  1094→}
  1095→
  1096→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1097→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1098→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1099→	if err != nil {
  1100→		return 0
  1101→	}
  1102→	return len(branches)
  1103→}

  1141→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1142→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1143→	var errs []error
  1144→
  1145→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1146→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1147→	if err == nil {
  1148→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1149→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1150→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1151→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1152→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1153→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1154→			}
  1155→		}
  1156→	}
  1157→
  1158→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1159→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1160→	if err == nil {
  1161→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1162→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1163→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1164→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1165→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1166→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1167→			}
  1168→		}
  1169→	}
  1170→
  1171→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1172→}
  1173→
  1174→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1175→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1176→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1177→	if err != nil {
  1178→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1179→	}
  1180→
  1181→	// Count states before removing
  1182→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1183→	if err != nil {
  1184→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1185→	}
  1186→	count := len(states)
  1187→
  1188→	// Remove the entire directory
  1189→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1190→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1191→	}
  1192→
  1193→	return count, nil
  1194→}
  1195→
  1196→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1197→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1198→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1199→	if err != nil {
  1200→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1201→	}
  1202→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1203→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1204→	}
  1205→	return nil
  1206→}

  1208→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1209→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1210→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1211→	if err != nil {

  1213→	}
  1214→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1215→		return 0, nil
  1216→	}
  1217→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1218→	return len(deleted), err

empty means file doesn't exist\n\t\tcreateParent   bool   // whether parent dir already exists\n\t}{\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:           \"zsh_new_file\",\n\t\t\trcFileRelPath:  \".zshrc\",\n\t\t\tcompletionLine: \"source <(entire completion zsh)\",\n\t\t\tcreateParent:   true,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:           \"zsh_existing_file\",\n\t\t\trcFileRelPath:  \".zshrc\",\n\t\t\tcompletionLine: \"source <(entire completion zsh)\",\n\t\t\tpreExisting:    \"# existing zshrc content\\n\",\n\t\t\tcreateParent:   true,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:           \"fish_no_parent_dir\",\n\t\t\trcFileRelPath:  filepath.Join(\".config\", \"fish\", \"config.fish\"),\n\t\t\tcompletionLine: \"entire completion fish | source\",\n\t\t\tcreateParent:   false,\n\t\t},\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tname:           \"fish_existing_dir\",\n\t\t\trcFileRelPath:  filepath.Join(\".config\", \"fish\", \"config.fish\"),\n\t\t\tcompletionLine: \"entire completion fish | source\",\n\t\t\tcreateParent:   true,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\thome := t.TempDir()\n\t\t\trcFile := filepath.Join(home, tt.rcFileRelPath)\n\n\t\t\tif tt.createParent {\n\t\t\t\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif tt.preExisting != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(tt.preExisting), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif err := installShellCompletion(rcFile, tt.completionLine); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"installShellCompletion() error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Verify the file was created and contains the stanza.\n\t\t\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"reading rc file: %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontent := string(data)\n\n\t\t\t// Verify stanza markers are present\n\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(content, \"# BEGIN \"+stanzaName) {\n\t\t\t\tt.Error(\"rc file missing stanza BEGIN marker\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(content, \"# END \"+stanzaName) {\n\t\t\t\tt.Error(\"rc file missing stanza END marker\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(content, tt.completionLine) {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"rc file missing completion line %q\", tt.completionLine)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Verify stanza can be found\n\t\t\tversion, body, found := FindStanza(content, stanzaName)\n\t\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"stanza not found after install\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif version != stanzaVersion {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"stanza version = %d, want %d\", version, stanzaVersion)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif body != tt.completionLine {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"stanza body = %q, want %q\", body, tt.completionLine)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Verify pre-existing content is preserved\n\t\t\tif tt.preExisting != \"\" && !strings.Contains(content, strings.TrimRight(tt.preExisting, \"\\n\")) {\n\t\t\t\tt.Error(\"pre-existing content was overwritten\")\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Verify parent directory exists.\n\t\t\tinfo, err := os.Stat(filepath.Dir(rcFile))\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"stat parent dir: %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !info.IsDir() {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatal(\"parent path is not a directory\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallShellCompletion_MigratesLegacy(t *testing.T) {\n\thome := t.TempDir()\n\trcFile := filepath.Join(home, \".zshrc\")\n\tlegacy := \"export PATH=/usr/bin\\n\\n\" + legacyCompletionComment + \"\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(legacy), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := installShellCompletion(rcFile, \"source <(entire completion zsh)\"); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"installShellCompletion() error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tcontent := string(data)\n\n\t// Legacy comment should be gone\n\tif strings.Contains(content, legacyCompletionComment) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"legacy comment should be removed after migration\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Stanza should be present\n\tversion, _, found := FindStanza(content, stanzaName)\n\tif !found {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"stanza should be present after migration\")\n\t}\n\tif version != stanzaVersion {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"stanza version = %d, want %d\", version, stanzaVersion)\n\t}\n\n\t// Pre-existing content preserved\n\tif !strings.Contains(content, \"export PATH=/usr/bin\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"pre-existing content should be preserved\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestCompletionStatus(t *testing.T) {\n\thome := t.TempDir()\n\n\t// Missing file\n\trcFile := filepath.Join(home, \".zshrc\")\n\tif status := completionStatus(rcFile); status != statusMissing {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"missing file: got %d, want statusMissing\", status)\n\t}\n\n\t// Empty file\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(\"\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif status := completionStatus(rcFile); status != statusMissing {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"empty file: got %d, want statusMissing\", status)\n\t}\n\n\t// Legacy format\n\tlegacy := legacyCompletionComment + \"\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(legacy), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif status := completionStatus(rcFile); status != statusLegacy {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"legacy format: got %d, want statusLegacy\", status)\n\t}\n\n\t// Current stanza\n\tcurrent := \"# BEGIN entire-cli (v1)\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n# END entire-cli\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(current), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif status := completionStatus(rcFile); status != statusCurrent {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"current stanza: got %d, want statusCurrent\", status)\n\t}\n\n\t// Stale stanza (older version)\n\tstale := \"# BEGIN entire-cli (v0)\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n# END entire-cli\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(stale), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif status := completionStatus(rcFile); status != statusStale {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"stale stanza: got %d, want statusStale\", status)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRemoveShellCompletion(t *testing.T) {\n\thome := t.TempDir()\n\trcFile := filepath.Join(home, \".zshrc\")\n\n\t// Remove from missing file — no error, not modified\n\tmodified, err := removeShellCompletion(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"removeShellCompletion on missing file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif modified {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not be modified when file doesn't exist\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove stanza format\n\tstanza := \"export A=1\\n\\n# BEGIN entire-cli (v1)\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n# END entire-cli\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(stanza), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tmodified, err = removeShellCompletion(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"removeShellCompletion: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif !modified {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should be modified when stanza is removed\")\n\t}\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif strings.Contains(string(data), \"# BEGIN entire-cli\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"stanza should be removed from file\")\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(string(data), \"export A=1\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"other content should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove legacy format\n\tlegacy := \"export B=2\\n\\n\" + legacyCompletionComment + \"\\nsource <(entire completion zsh)\\n\"\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(legacy), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tmodified, err = removeShellCompletion(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"removeShellCompletion legacy: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif !modified {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should be modified when legacy is removed\")\n\t}\n\tdata, err = os.ReadFile(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif strings.Contains(string(data), legacyCompletionComment) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"legacy comment should be removed\")\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(string(data), \"export B=2\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"other content should be preserved\")\n\t}\n\n\t// No completion present — not modified\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(rcFile, []byte(\"export C=3\\n\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tmodified, err = removeShellCompletion(rcFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"removeShellCompletion no-op: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif modified {\n\t\tt.Error(\"should not be modified when no completion present\")\n\t}\n}"
}

> AGENT

The problem is that when `inStanza` is true and the line is NOT the end marker, the code currently falls through and does nothing (which is correct — it skips the body line). But when `inStanza` is true and line IS the end marker, it sets `inStanza = false` but then the comment says "Skip stanza body lines when inStanza" which is misleading, and the end line is also correctly skipped because `result` isn't appended to.

Actually, wait — there's a bug. When `!inStanza` is false (i.e., `inStanza` is true) and `trimmed == endLine`, we enter the `else if` branch and set `inStanza = false`. But the line is not added to `result`, which is correct. However, when `inStanza` is true and `trimmed != endLine`, we fall through to the comment and don't add to `result`, which is also correct. But when `!inStanza` is true, we either match the begin prefix (and `continue`) or append to `result`. Then the comment line executes after that — but `result` was already appended. So the logic is actually fine.

Let me verify by running the tests.

The lint error was about the string `"export FOO=bar\n"` appearing 3 times (as standalone content variables). These are in test backtick strings vs regular strings. The goconst lint flags the backtick multiline strings that start with `export FOO=bar`. Since these are test strings, the simplest fix is to add a nolint comment. But actually, looking more carefully at the lint output:

```
cmd/entire/cli/stanza_test.go:32:13: string `export FOO=bar
` has 3 occurrences

Line 32 is `content := \`export FOO=bar\n\``. These multiline backtick strings are used as test fixtures with different surrounding content each time — extracting to a constant wouldn't improve readability. Let me just suppress the lint warning since these are in tests.
